Result: 获得了以一般Heun函数表达的精确解；低致密极限下恢复已知领先阶和次领先阶结果；Buchdahl极限下得到静态模式的规则性条件，并解析描述了动态模式在中心处的发散行为（振幅和振荡波矢的发散）。

Conclusion: 本文建立的精确解框架为研究强引力场中量子场的行为提供了新工具，特别是对于致密星内部的黑洞形成阈值附近标量场的动力学研究具有重要意义。

Abstract: We present for the first time the exact solution of the massive Klein-Gordon equation in the Schwarzschild star (perfect-fluid, uniform-density, spherically-symmetric star), including the non-minimal curvature-scalar coupling. The solution is expressed in terms of the general Heun function. A geometry-induced algebraic coordinate transformation reveals a hidden Fuchsian structure that underlies the exact solvability. Known leading- and next-to-leading-order results are recovered in the low-compactness limit. In the Buchdahl limit, we derive a regularity condition for static modes and describe analytically the divergence in amplitude and oscillation wave vector of dynamic modes as they approach the pressure singularity at the center of the star.

</details>


### [191] [Deriving effective descriptions and signal predictions for dynamical gravitational systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19435)
*Steven B. Giddings,Madhur Mehta*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于边界空腔作用的有效场论方法，用于描述双星等引力系统的辐射，并展示了如何通过空腔边界作用计算可观测的波形和相位偏移，旨在系统性地推导经典黑洞行为修正对引力波信号的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主要动机是建立一种系统性的方法，来推断经典黑洞行为的修正（如量子力学一致性所要求的黑洞演化修正，或其他新物理模型）对引力波信号的影响，特别是从旋近阶段累积的相位偏移对小效应的敏感性。

Method: 采用基于边界空腔作用（cavity action）的有效描述方法，在包裹单个天体的空腔边界上定义动力学，替代传统的世界线有效场论方法，从而规避截断依赖问题。文中以标量辐射为主要示例，并概述了向引力波情境的扩展。

Result: 给出了黑洞及黑洞行为修正简单模型的空腔描述示例，展示了空腔有效描述如何与可观测量的详细波形和累积相位偏移联系起来。

Conclusion: 边界空腔有效作用方法为系统性地研究黑洞行为修正对引力波信号的影响提供了新途径，相位偏移在旋近阶段对微小新效应具有灵敏度，为后续引力波应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: We investigate top-down derivations of effective descriptions for radiation from gravitational systems such as binaries. With a specified cutoff prescription, one can derive worldline effective field theories, but the cutoff dependence also complicates their description. We investigate a related effective approach, based on parameterizing dynamics in terms of an action on the boundaries of cavities encompassing individual bodies. We give examples of such cavity descriptions for black holes and for simple models for modifications of their behavior. We also show how cavity effective descriptions connect to observable quantities -- detailed wave profiles, and importantly, accumulated phase shift of emitted signals. A primary motivation is to have a systematic approach to inferring effects of modification of classical black hole behavior, such as those motivated by the need for black hole evolution to be consistent with quantum mechanics, or by other models for new BH behavior, on gravitational wave signals; the latter phase shifts have in particular been argued to provide sensitivity to small new effects from the inspiral phase. To illustrate basic principles and methods, this paper largely focuses on examples with scalar radiation, but we outline extension of the analysis to gravitational wave contexts.

</details>


### [192] [Dynamical Tidal Response of Neutron Stars: from Effective Field Theory to Gravitational Waveforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19446)
*Thomas Apostolidis,Valerio De Luca,Leonardo Gualtieri,Takuya Katagiri,Paolo Pani,Luca Santoni*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了中子星在全相对论框架下二阶频率的动力学潮汐响应，通过世界线有效场论与相对论性恒星模型微扰论相结合，推导了外部时变场诱导的潮汐形变（含通用对数跑动项）。首次给出了动力学潮汐效应对双致密星系统保守动力学和引力波信号的完整领头阶修正，发现相对论效应不能完全由模式激发描述，动力学Love数在低致密度时显著增强，尽管形式上出现在8阶后牛顿阶，但在晚期旋近中贡献不可忽略。第三代探测器（如爱因斯坦望远镜）可测量动力学Love数，忽略这些效应会导致静态Love数推断偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有引力波模型对中子星潮汐效应的处理多局限于静态或绝热近似，而真实双星系统中潮汐场是时变的，动力学效应在高精度建模中不可忽略。本文旨在填补全相对论框架下动力学潮汐响应到二阶频率的理论空白，并评估其对引力波探测的影响。

Method: 结合世界线有效场论（EFT）与相对论性恒星结构微扰论，采用维度正规化进行匹配，推导动力学潮汐响应函数，包括对数跑动项和有限项。利用Fisher矩阵分析评估第三代探测器对动力学Love数的测量能力。

Result: 首次得到动力学潮汐修正的完整领头阶结果；发现相对论动力学效应不能仅由模式激发描述；动力学Love数在低致密度时显著大于静态值；动力学效应在8PN阶进入波形相位但贡献不可忽略；Fisher分析表明爱因斯坦望远镜可测量动力学Love数，忽略则导致静态Love数显著偏差。

Conclusion: 动力学潮汐效应对于未来高精度引力波建模至关重要，特别在第三代探测器时代，必须将其纳入波形模板以避免对中子星状态方程推断的系统性偏差。

Abstract: We investigate the fully relativistic dynamical tidal response of neutron stars up to second order in the frequency. Combining the worldline effective field theory for extended gravitating bodies with perturbation theory of relativistic stellar models, we derive the tidal deformation induced by an external time-dependent field, including a universal logarithmic running term. In the effective theory, we work in dimensional regularization and, through a consistent matching procedure, obtain for the first time the complete leading-order dynamical tidal corrections to both the conservative dynamics and the gravitational-wave signal of compact binaries, including the scheme-dependent finite terms in addition to the running. We show that, in the relativistic regime, dynamical effects cannot be fully captured by mode excitations alone. The magnitude of the additional contribution depends on the stellar compactness, the equation of state, and the running term. Dynamical Love numbers are significantly enhanced with respect to their static counterparts for relatively small compactness. As a result, although they formally enter the gravitational-wave phase at 8th post-Newtonian order, dynamical tidal effects yield a non-negligible contribution during the late inspiral. Using a Fisher-matrix analysis, we show that third-generation detectors such as the Einstein Telescope could measure dynamical Love numbers for a range of neutron-star masses and equations of state. Conversely, neglecting these effects can lead to significant biases in the inference of static Love numbers, and hence on the nuclear equation of state. Our results highlight the importance of dynamical tidal effects for high-precision gravitational-wave modeling with future detectors.

</details>


### [193] [Exact vacuum FLRW solutions in $q$-deformed Brans-Dicke cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19563)
*Salih Kibaroğlu,Mustafa Senay*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究Brans-Dicke引力的一种$q$-变形推广，在平坦FLRW时空中引入耦合函数的变形，修改了有效引力强度并得到推广的Friedmann方程。在无物质情形下，获得了尺度因子和Brans-Dicke标量场的精确解析解，并将标量贡献重构为有效流体。状态方程参数和减速参数仅依赖于Brans-Dicke耦合常数$ω$和变形函数，使得标量扇区可在参数空间的受限区域内模拟辐射、物质或暗能量行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将$q$-变形（量子群/非对易几何相关思想）引入Brans-Dicke引力理论，探索耦合函数变形对标量-张量引力动力学的影响，寻找更丰富的宇宙学行为。

Method: 在平坦FLRW度规下，引入依赖于标量场的变形耦合函数修改Brans-Dicke作用量；推导推广的Friedmann方程；在无物质（真空）情形下求解尺度因子和标量场的精确解析解；将标量场贡献重新解释为有效理想流体，计算其状态方程参数和减速参数。

Result: 得到了精确解析解；发现标量有效流体的状态方程参数$w_{\text{eff}}$和减速参数$q$均为常数，且仅由$ω$和变形函数决定；在参数空间的特定区域，标量扇区可分别模拟辐射（$w_{\text{eff}}=1/3$）、物质（$w_{\text{eff}}=0$）或暗能量（$w_{\text{eff}}=-1$）行为。

Conclusion: $q$-变形Brans-Dicke引力在无物质情形下可产生丰富的有效流体行为，标量场本身即可充当辐射、物质或暗能量的角色，无需引入额外场。这为统一描述不同宇宙时期提供了一种理论框架。

Abstract: We study a $q$-deformed extension of Brans-Dicke gravity in a spatially flat Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker space-time. The deformation enters through a coupling function that modifies the effective gravitational strength and leads to generalized Friedmann equations. In the matter-free sector, we obtain exact analytic solutions for the scale factor and the Brans-Dicke scalar field, and recast the scalar contribution as an effective fluid. We show that the corresponding equation-of-state parameter and the deceleration parameter are constants and depend only on the Brans-Dicke coupling $ω$ and the deformation function, allowing the scalar sector to mimic radiation-, matter-, or dark-energy-like behavior for a restricted region of parameter space.

</details>


### [194] [Graviton Floor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19757)
*Himeka Matsuo,Asuka Ito,Kazunori Kohri,Teruaki Suyama,Ryutaro Tomomatsu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了宇宙光子背景在银河系和耀变体喷流中的磁场作用下转化为引力子的过程，发现耀变体喷流贡献占主导，形成的引力子背景将成为高频引力波探测器搜索新物理时的"引力子本底"，类似于中微子本底。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙中充斥着从射电波到伽马射线的宇宙光子背景，研究这些光子在磁场作用下转化为引力子的过程，评估其对高频引力波探测器的影响。

Method: 研究考虑了银河系和耀变体喷流中的背景磁场，分析光子背景转化为引力子的效率及贡献大小。

Result: 引力子背景主要来自耀变体喷流的贡献，而非银河系。

Conclusion: 该引力子背景构成了高频引力波探测器在搜索新物理时不可忽视的"引力子本底"，类似于中微子本底对暗物质探测的影响。

Abstract: It has been observed that the Universe is permeated by the cosmic photon background, ranging from radio waves to gamma rays. We investigate the conversion of the photon background into gravitons in the presence of background magnetic fields in the Milky Way Galaxy and in blazar jets. We find that the resulting graviton background is dominated by the contribution generated in blazar jets. Importantly, this graviton background constitutes a graviton floor for high-frequency gravitational wave detectors searching for new physics, analogous to the neutrino floor.

</details>


### [195] [Polarization-Dependent Photon Propagation, Quasinormal Modes, and Gravitational Lensing in Higher-Curvature Effective Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19773)
*Takamasa Kanai*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在有效场论框架下研究了高阶曲率修正对光子传播的影响，重点关注极化依赖的光子轨迹修正及其在强引力场中的观测效应。基于几何光学近似推导了有效度规，分析了光子球偏移、eikonal极限下的准正则模以及引力透镜偏转角，发现超越广义相对论的效应会在准正则模谱和强引力透镜观测中显现，并为约束有效场论提供了可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究高阶曲率修正（超越广义相对论的贡献）如何影响光子在强引力场中的传播，特别是极化依赖的修正效应，并考察这些修正是否可通过天文观测（准正则模和强引力透镜）加以约束。

Method: 采用有效场论框架，在静态球对称时空（Schwarzschild和Reissner-Nordström背景）下，利用几何光学近似推导光子传播的有效度规，研究极化依赖的光子球偏移；在eikonal极限下计算准正则模，并分析极化模式依赖性；进一步计算包含极化依赖修正的引力透镜偏转角。

Result: 明确了高阶曲率修正如何具体影响准正则模谱和强引力透镜观测信号，揭示了极化模式在这些观测量中的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 高阶曲率修正导致的极化依赖效应在准正则模和强引力透镜可观测信号中可被识别，这为利用天文观测约束有效场论提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We investigate the impact of higher-curvature corrections on photon propagation within an effective field theory framework and explore their observational consequences in strong gravitational fields. In particular, we consider polarization-dependent modifications to photon trajectories induced by higher-order curvature terms and analyze their effects in static and spherically symmetric spacetimes, focusing on Schwarzschild and Reissner-Nordström backgrounds.
  Using the geometrical optics approximation, we derive the effective metric governing photon propagation and study the resulting shifts in the photon sphere. Based on this modified propagation, we compute the quasinormal modes in the eikonal limit and examine their dependence on the polarization modes. We further analyze gravitational lensing observables, focusing on the deflection angle, incorporating the polarization-dependent corrections.
  Our results clarify how contributions from beyond-general-relativity effects manifest in both quasinormal mode spectra and strong gravitational lensing observables. These findings further suggest the possibility of placing meaningful constraints on effective field theories.

</details>


### [196] [Photon surfaces extension in general spherical dust collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19809)
*Roberto Giambò,Camilla Lucamarini*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将光子曲面的分析从零能量（marginally bound）情况推广到非零能量函数k(x)的一般Lemaître-Tolman-Bondi (LTB)球对称尘埃塌缩模型。通过将光子曲面条件重铸为非自治一阶动力系统，证明了外光子球面r=3M进入塌缩尘埃云的唯一物理扩展是由径向零测地线生成的类零曲面，并建立了光子曲面到达中心奇点当且仅当奇点是裸的这一结论，将已知的边际束缚结果推广至一般情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在之前的工作中，作者已经分析了marginally bound（k(x)=0）情况下LTB模型的光子曲面。本文的目的是将此分析推广到更一般的、非边际束缚的LTB模型（k(x)≠0），以检验先前建立的裸奇点与光子曲面之间的对应关系是否在一般情况下仍然成立，从而深化对球对称尘埃塌缩中因果结构与可观测信号之间联系的理解。

Method: 从LTB度规出发，考虑k(x)≠0的一般情况，推导光子曲面方程。利用动力系统方法，将类时光子曲面的几何条件重铸为一阶非自治动力系统。由于k(x)≠0时LTB演化方程无法封闭积分，借助文献中的隐式解以及常微分方程的比较定理进行分析。结合一般LTB模型的因果结构已知结果，论证光子曲面的行为与奇点裸露性之间的等价性。

Result: 1）外部光子球面r=3M进入塌缩尘埃云的唯一物理可接受的扩展是类零超曲面，由出射径向零测地线生成。2）光子曲面到达中心奇点当且仅当该奇点是裸的（即存在类零/类时测地线从奇点延伸到无穷远）。3）将marginally bound情况下的已知图像成功推广至一般LTB模型。

Conclusion: 在一般LTB球对称尘埃塌缩中，光子曲面的最终结构（是否到达中心）与奇点的裸露性之间存在严格的一一对应。这一结构性二分法（裸vs覆盖）对早期黑洞阴影的可观测特征具有潜在意义，为检验宇宙审查假设提供了可能的观测途径。

Abstract: We extend the analysis of photon surfaces in spherical dust collapse to the general, non-marginally bound case, i.e.\ allowing the \textit{energy function} $k(x)$ of the Lemaître--Tolman--Bondi (LTB) model to be non-zero. Starting from the dynamical-systems formulation developed in our previous work for the marginally bound case (arXiv:2509.01368), we derive the photon surface equations for the LTB metric with $k(x)\neq 0$, and we recast the geometric condition for a timelike hypersurface to be a photon surface as a non-autonomous first-order dynamical system. Even though the LTB evolution equation does not integrate in closed form when $k(x)\neq 0$, the implicit solutions available in the literature, together with comparison theorems for ordinary differential equations, are sufficient to show that the only physically acceptable extension of the exterior photon sphere $r=3M$ into the collapsing dust cloud is a null hypersurface, generated by outgoing radial null geodesics. Combining this fact with the known results on the causal structure of the general LTB model, we then establish that the photon surface reaches the central singularity if and only if the singularity is naked, thereby extending to the general case the picture already known in the marginally bound regime. The structural dichotomy between the naked and covered end states is also discussed in connection with possible observational signatures in the early-time evolution of black-hole shadows.

</details>


### [197] [Covariant Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff equations in Energy-Momentum Squared Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19879)
*Eduardo Bittencourt,Mariam Campbell,Peter K. S. Dunsby,Sergio E. Jorás*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在能量-动量平方引力框架下，利用协变1+1+2半 tetrad 形式体系研究静态球对称恒星结构。通过将非线性物质修正重新解释为有效完美流体，得到与经典 TOV 方程形式相同的恒星平衡方程，并简化为自治平面动力学系统。在线性物态方程下找回广义相对论基准，识别出与 GR 精确、渐近或分段等价的扇区，同时发现尘埃构型下平面约化失效需考虑三维流。匹配条件为有效压强在恒星表面为零，不必等价于物理压强为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将广义相对论的恒星结构研究拓展到能量-动量平方引力（EMSG）这一修正引力理论中，利用协变形式体系系统分析非线性物质修正对恒星平衡与稳定性的影响。

Method: 采用协变1+1+2半 tetrad 形式体系；将非线性物质项重新参数化为有效完美流体；引入有效变量使平衡方程保持 TOV 形式；推导结构方程并约化为平面动力系统；通过临界点分析定性描述相空间。

Result: 非线性物质修正可映射为有效完美流体，使平衡方程保持标准 TOV 形式；平面动力系统可描述大部分扇区；在线性物态方程下恢复 GR 基准；尘埃构型平面约化失效需用三维流；匹配条件为有效压强 p_eff(R)=0，可不同于物理压强为零。

Conclusion: 能量-动量平方引力中的恒星解可通过有效流体重构为类似 GR 的形式，但匹配条件和某些物态（如尘埃）下动力学行为存在本质差异，需更高维描述。

Abstract: We study static, spherically symmetric stellar configurations in an extended class of Energy--Momentum Squared Gravity using the covariant \(1+1+2\) semi-tetrad formalism. For perfect physical fluids, we show that the nonlinear matter corrections can be reinterpreted as an effective perfect fluid, so that the stellar equilibrium equations retain the standard Tolman--Oppenheimer--Volkoff form when written in terms of effective variables. The resulting covariant structure equations are formulated in both metric and dimensionless variables and, whenever an effective closure relation exists, reduce to an autonomous planar dynamical system. This provides a global qualitative description of the stellar phase space in terms of finite and asymptotic critical points.
  Specializing to linear physical equations of state, we recover the general relativistic benchmark and identify sectors that are exactly, asymptotically, or piecewise equivalent to general relativity, as well as sectors -- particularly dust configurations -- for which the planar reduction breaks down and the full three-dimensional covariant flow must be considered. We further recover the standard metric Tolman--Oppenheimer--Volkoff equation in terms of effective variables and show that, although the exterior spacetime remains Schwarzschild, the natural matching condition at the stellar surface is \(p_{\rm eff}(R)=0\), which need not coincide with \(p(R)=0\) for self-bound matter.

</details>


### [198] [Cosmological Constraints on Minimal Cubic Galileon Models in Teleparallel Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19905)
*Akbar Davlataliev,Abdurakhmon Nosirov,Odil Yunusov,Bobomurat Ahmedov,Jackson Levi Said*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在teleparallel引力框架下研究cubic Galileon宇宙学模型，利用Pantheon+超新星、宇宙时钟、SH0ES和BAO数据约束模型参数，发现quadratic势且固定b₁=2的模型在包含BAO数据时给出比ΛCDM更低的χ²_min，但BIC准则仍偏好ΛCDM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索标准ΛCDM范式之外解释晚期宇宙加速膨胀的理论框架，检验teleparallel引力框架下cubic Galileon模型的观测可行性。

Method: 考虑quadratic和exponential两种标量场势函数，构建四种代表性场景（两种势下b₁自由和b₁=2固定），利用Pantheon+ Ia型超新星样本、宇宙时钟测量、SH0ES信息和BAO数据对模型参数进行观测约束，并通过χ²_min、AIC和BIC准则比较不同场景的统计表现。

Result: Quadratic势且固定b₁=2的模型在包含BAO数据时给出比ΛCDM更低的χ²_min，AIC准则下支持度相当；但BIC准则因扩展模型参数空间更大仍偏好ΛCDM。所有teleparallel cubic Galileon模型均能描述晚期膨胀历史，但统计偏好取决于势函数选择和b₁是否固定/自由。

Conclusion: Teleparallel cubic Galileon宇宙学在现象学上仍然可行，但关于缓解Hubble tension的主张需要进一步一致性检验。

Abstract: Cubic Galileon cosmological models provide a well-motivated framework for investigating late-time cosmic acceleration beyond the standard $Λ$CDM paradigm. In this work, we study observational constraints on cubic Galileon models within the teleparallel gravity framework, where deviations from the standard teleparallel equivalent of general relativity are encoded through the model parameter $b_1$. We consider two scalar-field potentials, namely quadratic and exponential potentials, and analyze four representative scenarios: quadratic and exponential potentials with $b_1$ treated as a free parameter, together with the corresponding cases in which $b_1=2$ is fixed. Using the $\text{Pantheon}^+$ Type Ia supernova sample, cosmic chronometer measurements, SH0ES information, and baryon acoustic oscillation data, we constrain the cosmological and model parameters and compare the observational viability of the different scenarios. We find that the considered teleparallel cubic Galileon models can accommodate the late-time expansion history, although the statistical preference depends on the choice of potential and on whether $b_1$ is fixed or varied. In particular, the fixed-$b_1$ model with a quadratic potential provides the most competitive fit among the Galileon scenarios when BAO data are included, showing a lower $χ^2_{\min}$ than $Λ$CDM and comparable support according to the AIC criterion. However, the BIC criterion continues to favor the minimal $Λ$CDM model because of the larger parameter space of the extended models. These results suggest that teleparallel cubic Galileon cosmologies remain phenomenologically viable, while a stronger claim regarding the Hubble tension requires further consistency tests.

</details>


### [199] [On the Plebanski Formulation with Energy Momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20042)
*Jack C. M. Hughes,Joudy F. Jamal Beek,Fedor V. Kusmartsev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将度规表述中的物质源（能量-动量张量）系统地转化为Plebanski形式中所需的自对偶/反自对偶源项的方法，并通过Kulkarni-Nomizu乘积将无迹能量-动量张量提升为代数曲率的(1,1)分量，再提取其手征分量。该方法重现了Krasnov的定义，并证明手征场方程通过手征Bianchi恒等式导出能量守恒律。以球对称电磁应力-能量张量为应用，得到了Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Plebanski形式中物质耦合不如度规形式直接，因为能量-动量张量是对称的，而Plebanski变量自然取值于2-形式的自对偶/反自对偶Hodge分解中。需要一种系统的方法将度规物质源翻译为Plebanski场方程所需的反自对偶源项。

Method: 通过Kulkarni-Nomizu乘积将无迹能量-动量张量$\hat T_{\mu\nu}$提升为代数曲率空间的(1,1)分量，然后提取其手征分量（自对偶和反自对偶部分），从而构造Plebanski物质源$T^i$。

Result: 该构造重现了Krasnov用自对偶基$\Sigma^i$和$\hat T_{\mu\nu}$给出的$T^i$定义。证明了物质耦合手征场方程通过手征Bianchi恒等式$d^A F^i=0$导出能量守恒律$\nabla_\mu T^{\mu\nu}=0$。应用于球对称电磁应力-能量张量时，反自对偶部分的场方程给出Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter解。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种将度规物质源系统地翻译为Plebanski场方程所需的反自对偶源项的系统性处方，为Plebanski形式中的物质耦合建立了清晰的桥梁。

Abstract: In Plebanski's formulation the coupling of matter is less direct than in the metric formulation since the energy-momentum tensor $T_{μν}$ is symmetric, while the Plebanski variables are naturally valued in the self-dual/anti-self-dual Hodge decomposition of 2-forms. An explicit construction of the Plebanski matter source $T^i$ is obtained by lifting the trace-free energy-momentum tensor $\hat T_{μν}$ into the $(1,1)$ component of the algebraic curvature space using the Kulkarni-Nomizu product, and then extracting its chiral components. This construction reproduces the definition for $T^i$ in terms of the self-dual basis $Σ^i$ and $\hat T_{μν}$ introduced by Krasnov. We also verify that the matter-coupled chiral field equations imply the usual conservation law $\nabla_μT^{μν}=0$ through the chiral Bianchi identity $d^A F^i=0$. As an application, the construction is applied to a spherically symmetric electromagnetic stress-energy tensor, where the anti-self-dual part of the matter-coupled Plebanski field equations yields the Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter solution. The result gives a systematic prescription for translating metric matter sources into the anti-self-dual source terms required by the Plebanski field equations.

</details>


### [200] [Regular Black Holes from Anisotropic Source with Hydrodynamic Equation of State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20109)
*Hassan Firouzjahi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了由各向异性能动张量驱动的规则黑洞解。通过将能动张量分解为各向同性和各向异性分量，假设流体动力学状态方程 P=P(ρ)，得到了多种已知和新奇的规则黑洞度规。发现 P(ρ) 曲线在远处存在根和极大值，导致声速平方变号并可能引发流体动力学不稳定性。亚光速约束排除了能量密度指数衰减的模型，并建立了能量条件违反点、压力根点和极大点之间的普适层级关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索由各向异性物质源产生的规则黑洞解，理解其内部几何趋近dS度规的机制，并分析不同状态方程下黑洞解的存在性与物理性质。

Method: 将能动张量分解为各向同性和各向异性部分，采用流体动力学状态方程 P=P(ρ)，求解球对称规则黑洞解。分析 P(ρ) 的根、极大值及声速平方 c_s² 的符号变化，施加亚光速约束，建立能量条件违反与热力学量极值点之间的层级关系。

Result: 获得了多种已知和新奇的规则黑洞度规；发现 P(ρ) 在远处有根和极大值，c_s² 在压力极大点处变号表明潜在不稳定性；亚光速约束排除了指数衰减能量密度模型；建立了强能量条件违反点、压力根点和极大点之间的普适层级顺序。

Conclusion: 各向异性源规则黑洞解的流体动力学稳定性受状态方程形式约束，声速变号指示不稳定性，亚光速限制严重制约模型参数，且存在普适的物理量层级顺序。

Abstract: We study regular black hole solutions sourced by an anisotropic energy momentum tensor. It is well known that the geometry of the interior of a spherically symmetric regular black hole approaches the dS metric. Having decomposed the energy momentum tensor into its isotropic and anisotropic components, we assume a hydrodynamic equation of state, $P= P(ρ)$, for the pressure, and look for spherically symmetric, regular black hole solutions. We consider different forms of $ P(ρ)$ which yield the previously known regular black hole solutions, as well as various new metrics. We show that the profile of $ P(ρ)$ has a root and a maximum as it approaches $0^+$ at large distances. Consequently, the square of the sound speed of perturbations, $c_s^2$, changes sign at the point where $P$ reaches its maximum, indicating a potential hydrodynamic instability. In addition, imposing the subluminal bound on $c_s$ puts strong constraints on the model parameters, excluding models in which the energy density has an exponential fall off. We establish a universal hierarchy among the relative positions at which the strong energy condition is violated, at which $P$ has its root, and at which $P$ attains its maximum.

</details>


### [201] [The auxiliary-metric formulation of Born-Infeld New Massive Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20247)
*Bayram Tekin*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: BINMG通过辅助度规将New Massive Gravity推广至所有曲率阶，其多项式形式简化了分析和计算，并揭示了Fierz-Pauli调谐的自然出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将New Massive Gravity (NMG) 推广至曲率的所有阶，从而得到一个更完整的引力理论，同时简化其数学处理和物理解析。

Method: 引入独立辅助度规$q_{\mu\nu}$，其代数运动方程$q_{\mu\nu}=g_{\mu\nu}+\frac{\sigma}{m^2}G_{\mu\nu}(g)$精确恢复行列式作用量的正则分支。使用密度化变量$P^{\mu\nu}=\sqrt{-q}\,q^{\mu\nu}$将三维作用量变为多项式形式，其中所有导数依赖由耦合项$P^{\mu\nu}G_{\mu\nu}(g)$承载。

Result: 唯一真空只需一行推导得出；二次作用量产生单个Pauli-Fierz有质量自旋-2场，且Fierz-Pauli调谐是自然生成而非人为施加；在局域AdS背景上，守恒荷、BTZ质量和角动量、中心荷以及熵均简化为Einstein结果乘以公共因子。

Conclusion: 该表述显著简化了BINMG的分析，并为在正确变量中隔离非线性自由度问题提供了框架，将完整的Dirac计数留给后续工作。

Abstract: Born-Infeld New Massive Gravity (BINMG) completes New Massive Gravity to all orders in curvature through the determinant of the metric shifted by the Einstein tensor. We recast it with an independent auxiliary metric $q_{μν}$, whose algebraic equation of motion $q_{μν}=g_{μν}+\fracσ{m^2}G_{μν}(g)$ recovers the determinant action exactly on the regular branch and resums the infinite curvature series into a single relation. In the densitized variable $P^{μν}=\sqrt{-q}\,q^{μν}$ the three-dimensional action is polynomial, with all derivative dependence carried by the coupling $P^{μν}G_{μν}(g)$. The formulation makes known properties follow with substantially less algebra: the unique vacuum follows in one line, and the quadratic action yields a single Pauli-Fierz massive spin-2 field with the Fierz-Pauli tuning generated rather than imposed. On locally AdS backgrounds the conserved charges, BTZ mass and angular momentum, central charge, and entropy reduce to the Einstein results times a common factor. The formulation also isolates the nonlinear degree-of-freedom problem in the right variables, leaving the full Dirac count to separate work.

</details>


### [202] [An explicit and differentiable Wilson-Daubechies-Meyer transform for gravitational-wave data analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20269)
*Avi Vajpeyi,Giorgio Mentasti,Quentin Baghi,Ollie Burke,Lorenzo Speri*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 wdm_transform，一个开源的 Python 包，实现了 Wilson-Daubechies-Meyer (WDM) 小波包时频变换，提供 NumPy 和 JAX 后端，支持 GPU 加速，并在 LISA 银河双星示例中验证了 WDM 域似然与频域后验的数值等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WDM 时频变换已在引力波天文学中广泛应用，但缺乏一个自包含、数学明确的参考实现，尤其在希望将变换集成到现代 Python 和 JAX 推理工作流中的实践者中更为突出。

Method: 开发了 wdm_transform 开源 Python 包，实现 WDM 小波包时频变换，提供 NumPy 和 JAX 两个后端；正向和逆向变换经验证达到浮点精度；JAX 后端支持 GPU 加速，可在几十毫秒内处理百万点数据流。

Result: 在 LISA 银河双星解析示例中，验证了 WDM 域似然与频域后验在共享平稳噪声模型下的数值等价性。JAX 后端实现了 GPU 加速的百万点数据毫秒级变换。

Conclusion: 该工作为 WDM 时频铺砌的系统优化铺平了道路，特别适用于未来探测器面临非平稳噪声、随机背景和数据缺口等挑战，并为引力波数据分析所需的替代时频表示的直接比较提供了基础。

Abstract: The Wilson-Daubechies-Meyer (WDM) time-frequency transform has been widely used in gravitational-wave astronomy, yet a self-contained, mathematically explicit reference for practitioners remains lacking. This is especially true for those wishing to adopt the transform in modern Python and JAX inference workflows. We present wdm_transform, an open-source Python package implementing the WDM wavelet-packet time-frequency transform, and document its mathematical foundations, statistical properties, and practical implementation for gravitational-wave data analysis. The package supplies NumPy and JAX backends, both transforms (forward and inverse) validated to floating-point precision, with the JAX backend enabling GPU-accelerated transforms of million-point data streams in tens of milliseconds. As a worked example, we verify that the WDM-domain likelihood reproduces frequency-domain posteriors for a resolved LISA galactic binary under a shared stationary noise model, confirming numerical equivalence of the two representations in that controlled setting. This work paves the way for systematic optimisation of WDM tilings, a particularly promising direction for the non-stationary noise, stochastic backgrounds, and data gaps anticipated in future detectors, and for direct comparisons with alternative time-frequency representations needed to meet the challenges of future gravitational-wave data analysis.

</details>


### [203] [Constitutive birefringence and critical curves in the rotating García--Díaz black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20284)
*Ariel Guzmán,Mohsen Fathi,J. R. Villanueva*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究旋转García–Díaz解中高频电磁波在非线性电动力学（NLED）耦合下的传播，发现NLED场作为光学介质导致双折射效应：Fresnel四次型分解为两个二次分支，各自定义有效光学度量并产生临界轮廓Γ₊和Γ₋，通过数值扫描量化了非线性耦合、自旋和观测者倾角对双折射分裂的影响，建立了局域NLED响应与偏振依赖临界结构之间的几何联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在爱因斯坦引力与NLED耦合的系统中，光传播不仅由时空度量的零锥决定，NLED场还充当光学介质改变物理光学锥。作者希望理解这种非线性电磁响应如何影响高频电磁波的传播，特别是双折射效应及其与引力理论的耦合。

Method: 从混合电磁势出发，在主标架上投影场F和激发P得到对齐标量E,B,D,H，通过映射(D,B)↦(E,H)重建与Maxwell理论相连的局域本构分支；将响应矩阵代入Fresnel特征问题，在微扰阶下将四次型分解为两个二次分支（各定义有效光学度量）；利用Carter型分离变量的Hamilton-Jacobi方程得到径向和角向势能、临界常数和临界族；将临界族投影到有限距离观测者的天球上得到两个临界轮廓Γ₊和Γ₋。

Result: 两个临界轮廓在Maxwell极限下重合，在NLED非线性本构响应激活时分裂。通过最大角分离、相对直径位移和归一化双折射宽度三个量化指标，数值扫描显示分裂由本构响应产生，被旋转重新分布，且在微扰域内对局域投影变化稳定。

Conclusion: 建立了局域NLED响应与观测者屏幕上偏振依赖临界结构之间的直接几何联系，为通过天文观测检验非线性电动力学效应提供了理论预测框架。

Abstract: We study high-frequency electromagnetic propagation in the rotating García--Díaz solution of Einstein gravity coupled to NLED. In this system, light is not governed only by the null cone of the spacetime metric, because the NLED field also behaves as an optical medium whose constitutive response determines the physical optical cones. Starting from the mixed electromagnetic potentials, we project the field $F$ and the excitation $P$ on a principal tetrad and obtain the aligned scalars $E$, $B$, $D$ and $H$. These scalars allow us to reconstruct the regular local constitutive branch connected with Maxwell theory through the map $(D,B)\mapsto(E,H)$. We then insert the resulting response matrix into the Fresnel characteristic problem. At the perturbative order considered here, the Fresnel quartic factorizes into two quadratic branches, each defining an effective optical metric. Both optical metrics admit Carter-type separation of the Hamilton--Jacobi equation and possess their own radial and angular potentials, critical constants and unstable critical families. By projecting these families onto the celestial sphere of a finite-distance observer, we obtain two critical contours, $Γ_+$ and $Γ_-$, which coincide in the Maxwell limit and split when the nonlinear constitutive response is active. We quantify this birefringent splitting through the maximum angular separation, the relative diameter shift and the normalized birefringent width. Numerical scans over the nonlinear coupling, spin and observer inclination show that the splitting is generated by the constitutive response, redistributed by rotation and stable under local projection changes within the perturbative domain. This provides a direct geometrical link between the local NLED response and a polarization-dependent critical structure on the observer screen.

</details>


### [204] [Impossibility of superluminal signalling rules out causal loops in conical spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20476)
*Maarten Grothus,V. Vilasini*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 论文解决了在d>1维闵可夫斯基时空中，无超光速信号原则（NSS）是否排除所有可操作检测的因果循环这一关键问题。结果表明，在包括d>1维闵可夫斯基时空在内的一类"锥形"时空中，NSS确实排除了所有可操作检测的因果循环（经典、量子及后量子理论中均成立），揭示了NSS与无因果循环原则之间的关系本质上依赖于时空几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PRL 129, 110401在(1+1)维闵可夫斯基时空中证明了存在不违反NSS的可操作检测因果循环的理论可能性。但这一结论是否适用于更高维时空（d>1）一直是一个悬而未决的关键问题。解决这一问题有助于理解相对论性原理与因果结构之间的深层关系。

Method: 论文通过理论分析，研究了一类"锥形"（conical）时空结构（包括d>1维闵可夫斯基时空），考察NSS原则对可操作检测因果循环的约束。分析覆盖了经典理论、量子理论以及后量子（post-quantum）理论框架。

Result: 发现在d>1维的锥形时空中，NSS原则确实排除了所有可操作检测的因果循环。这一结论在经典、量子及后量子理论中均成立。

Conclusion: NSS与无因果循环原则之间的关系并非普适，而是本质上依赖于时空的几何结构——在(1+1)维中NSS允许某些可检测因果循环，但在d>1维的锥形时空中NSS排除它们。

Abstract: In PRL 129, 110401 it was shown that it is theoretically possible to have operationally detectable causal loops without violating the principle of no superluminal signalling (NSS) in (1+1)-Minkowski spacetime. Whether or not such causal loops are also possible in $d > 1$ spatial dimensions, has remained a key open question. We resolve this question by showing that in a wide class of "conical" spacetimes, including Minkowski with d > 1, NSS does rule out all operationally detectable causal loops, in classical, quantum and post-quantum theories. This establishes that the relationship between the relativistic principles of NSS and no causal loops depends inherently on the geometry of spacetime.

</details>


### [205] [Propagation of Dirac spherical waves in the expanding universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20483)
*Karen Yagdjian*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文给出了膨胀宇宙中狄拉克方程球谐解的显式公式。初始解可以是类氢原子波函数或闵可夫斯基空间中的球面波，随后在具有德西特尺度的FLRW时空中传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究膨胀宇宙中狄拉克方程的球谐解，特别是从闵可夫斯基空间中的初始波函数（如类氢原子波函数）在FLRW时空中的演化行为。

Method: 推导了膨胀宇宙中狄拉克方程的球谐解的显式解析公式，初始条件包括类氢原子波函数和闵可夫斯基空间球面波。

Result: 得到了膨胀宇宙（德西特尺度FLRW时空）中狄拉克方程的球谐解的显式表达式。

Conclusion: 提供了膨胀宇宙背景下狄拉克方程球谐解的分析形式，为理解量子系统在膨胀时空中的演化提供了数学框架。

Abstract: The explicit formulas for the spherical solutions of the Dirac equation in the expanding universe are given. The initial value of the solution can be, in particular, a wave function of the hydrogen-like atom or a spherical wave in the Minkowski space, that then propagates in the Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker space-time, which is expanding with the de~Sitter scale

</details>


### [206] [Higher Lovelock Curvature Terms Favor Local Nakedness in Dust Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20540)
*Apratim Ganguly,Radouane Gannouji,Akshay Kumar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了更高阶Lovelock引力项在球对称尘埃坍缩中的作用，发现这些项不会恢复局部宇宙审查，反而促进了中心壳聚焦奇点的局部可见性。非临界维度的陷俘指数β_N和临界维度（D=2N+1）的奇点曲线向外张开条件决定了局部可见性，且可见奇点沿出射零射线为Królak强，在阈值处可达Tipler强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究更高阶Lovelock曲率项是否能在球对称尘埃坍缩中恢复局部宇宙审查机制，以及其对奇点可见性的影响。

Method: 通过分析非临界维度（D-1-2N>0）中表观视界曲线与奇点曲线的接近程度，定义陷俘指数β_N=(D-1)/(D-1-2N)；结合奇点曲线首次非零修正项r^ℓ，导出局部可见性条件ℓ<β_N；并分析临界奇数维分支（D=2N+1）中无表观视界形成的情况。

Result: 高阶Lovelock项不恢复宇宙审查，反而增强奇点局部可见性；非临界维度中增大N扩大产生中心出射零射线的初始数据类；临界维度中奇点曲线向外张开即保证局部可见性；可见奇点沿出射零射线为Królak强，阈值处达Tipler强度；束缚与非束缚坍缩中非临界指数不变。

Conclusion: 更高阶Lovelock引力项不能恢复局部宇宙审查，反而促进奇点局部可见性，且不同维度分支的可见性条件及奇点强度特征明确。

Abstract: We show that higher-curvature Lovelock terms do not restore local cosmic censorship in spherical dust collapse, but instead promote the local visibility of central shell-focusing singularities. On the collapse branch with positive highest-order Lovelock coefficient \(c_N\), the highest nonvanishing Lovelock order \(N\) controls both the near-singularity collapse and the formation of trapped surfaces. In noncritical dimensions, \(D-1-2N>0\), the apparent-horizon curve approaches the singularity curve with trapping exponent \(β_N=(D-1)/(D-1-2N)\). Comparing this scale with the first nonvanishing correction \(r^\ell\) to the singularity curve gives the local-visibility condition \(\ell<β_N\), provided the singularity curve opens outward. Thus increasing \(N\) enlarges the class of inhomogeneous initial data producing outgoing radial null rays from the central singularity. In the critical odd-dimensional branch, \(D=2N+1\), no apparent horizon forms sufficiently close to the center, so any outward opening of the singularity curve gives local visibility. The locally visible singularities are Królak-strong along the emerging null rays, with Tipler strength reached at threshold. For bound and unbound collapse, the noncritical exponents are unchanged: the energy function modifies the opening of the singularity curve, while in the critical branch it enters the leading terminal collapse velocity.

</details>

### [207] [sft-wick: A formalism and package for Feynman-diagram expansion and evaluation in stochastic field theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19480)
*Zheng Zhang*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍sft-wick，一个开源Python包，用于随机场理论中Feynman图表的自动生成与数值积分计算。该工具通过先枚举空间拓扑再路由分量指标的方式，避免了传统Wick收缩的阶乘爆炸问题，并自动处理响应场约束、因果条件等，结果与直接Langevin模拟在统计误差内一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机场动力学的路径积分形式中，微扰理论虽然系统化但Wick配对随阶数阶乘增长，且多分量、张量值顶点下的指标路由使组合爆炸问题更加严重。现有方法缺乏能自动生成拓扑分类、多重因子、耦合求和、符号及因果约束的完整图表表格的工具。

Method: sft-wick采用先枚举空间拓扑再路由分量指标的核心算法，避免逐个收缩的Wick展开。它针对给定作用量和可观测量，枚举拓扑不同的Feynman图，导出代数系数，并从用户提供的响应函数和累积量函数中数值计算图积分。枚举过程中自动施加响应场约束（如无响应-响应收缩、Ito约定、无因果响应环）。

Result: 预测与直接Langevin模拟结果在统计噪声范围内一致，验证了方法的正确性和数值精度。

Conclusion: sft-wick为随机场理论中的高阶微扰计算提供了高效、自动化的工具，解决了组合爆炸和指标路由难题，适用于多分量、多维度、张量耦合和非高斯噪声的复杂系统。

Abstract: When stochastic field dynamics are cast into a path-integral formulation, perturbation theory becomes systematic but the resulting expansion quickly grows combinatorially large. The setting targeted here includes multi-component, multi-dimensional fields with matrix propagators, tensor-valued couplings, and non-Gaussian driving noise specified by arbitrary $n$-point cumulants. Wick pairings grow factorially, and component indices must be routed through the tensor-valued vertices. The useful output is not a raw contraction list, but a diagram table: one entry per topology, with multiplicities, coupling sums, signs, and causal constraints resolved. We present sft-wick, an open-source Python package that constructs these diagram tables and computes their integrals numerically. Given an action and an observable, it enumerates topologically distinct Feynman diagrams, derives their algebraic coefficients, and evaluates the resulting diagram integrals from user-supplied response and cumulant functions. The core algorithm enumerates spatial topologies before routing component indices, avoiding contraction-by-contraction Wick expansion. Response-field constraints, including vanishing response-response contractions, the ito prescription, and the absence of causal response loops, are enforced during enumeration. Predictions are validated against direct Langevin simulation, agreeing to within the simulation's statistical noise.

</details>


### [208] [TorchNEP: Ultra-Efficient and Accurate Training of Neuroevolution Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19557)
*Yong-Chao Wu,Xiaoya Chang,Tero Mäkinen,Amin Esfandiarpour,Jian-Li Shao,Tapio Ala-Nissila,Zheyong Fan,Mikko Alava*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: TorchNEP是基于PyTorch实现的NEP框架，通过解析梯度、自适应优化和两阶段训练策略，将训练速度提升两个数量级以上，同时发现提升预测精度主要源于两阶段训练协议而非优化算法本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始NEP框架的训练策略计算成本高，限制了模型架构和训练协议的系统性探索。

Method: 提出了基于PyTorch的TorchNEP实现，结合解析梯度、自适应优化和两阶段训练策略。

Result: 训练加速超过两个数量级；两阶段训练协议是提升预测精度的主要因素；在力场和应力预测上持续改进，能量精度保持或提升；增加模型复杂度不一定提升预测性能。

Conclusion: TorchNEP为开发更准确、更鲁棒的机器学习原子间势提供了一个高效且灵活的训练框架。

Abstract: Neuroevolution Potential (NEP) is one of the most efficient machine-learned interatomic potential frameworks for large-scale atomistic simulations. However, its original training strategy remains computationally demanding, limiting systematic exploration of model architectures and training protocols. Here, we present TorchNEP, a PyTorch-based implementation of NEP that combines analytically derived gradients, adaptive optimization, and a two-stage training strategy. TorchNEP accelerates training by more than two orders of magnitude while maintaining full compatibility with existing NEP models. We further show that the improvement in predictive accuracy primarily originates from the two-stage training protocol rather than the optimization algorithm itself. Across diverse benchmark datasets, TorchNEP consistently improves force and stress predictions while maintaining comparable or improved energy accuracy. Benchmark evaluations on elemental and alloy systems demonstrate enhanced predictive performance for both atomic configurations and key materials properties. Furthermore, we show that increasing model complexity does not necessarily improve predictive performance despite reducing training errors. Overall, TorchNEP provides an efficient and flexible training framework for developing more accurate and robust machine-learned interatomic potentials.

</details>


### [209] [Machine-learned prediction of carbon interstitial clusters in diamond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19600)
*Xiaoya Chang,Arsalan Hashemi,Nima Ghafari Cherati,Mikko Karttunen,Ádám Gali,Tapio Ala-Nissila*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过主动学习构建了以碳自间隙原子为核心的数据集，并基准测试了三种机器学习原子间势能（GAP、NEP、MACE）相较于密度泛函理论在能量、力和迁移势垒上的表现。MACE能准确再现参考能量学和相对稳定性，而其他模型可能错误排序基态。利用验证后的势能进行退火分子动力学模拟，发现了从双间隙到八间隙的一系列此前未报道的碳间隙团簇，其中几个引入了作为色心感兴趣的在带隙内状态，并显示其亚稳定性由动力学可达路径而非能量排序决定。这些结果绘制了间隙缺陷景观并加速了量子技术的缺陷发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金刚石中光活性点缺陷是量子技术的核心，但生长和辐照过程中引入的碳自间隙原子会与它们竞争并形成新缺陷，其构型景观尚未被充分描绘，因为微小的能量差异控制着竞争极小值和路径。

Method: 通过主动学习构建间隙聚焦数据集，基准测试三种机器学习原子间势能（GAP、NEP、MACE）与密度泛函理论在能量、力和迁移势垒上的表现，并用验证后的势能进行退火分子动力学模拟。

Result: MACE准确再现参考能量学和相对稳定性；发现从双间隙到八间隙的一系列此前未报道的碳间隙团簇，其中几个引入在带隙内状态；亚稳定性由动力学可达路径而非能量排序决定。

Conclusion: 绘制了间隙缺陷景观，可加速量子技术的缺陷发现。

Abstract: Diamond hosts optically active point defects central to quantum technologies, yet the carbon self-interstitials introduced during growth and irradiation compete with them and form new defects whose configurational landscape is poorly charted, as subtle energy differences govern the competing minima and pathways. Here we build an interstitial-focused dataset by active learning and benchmark three machine-learning interatomic potentials -- GAP, NEP and the equivariant MACE -- against density functional theory for energies, forces and migration barriers. MACE reproduces the reference energetics and relative stabilities, whereas the others can misorder the ground states. Annealing molecular dynamics with the validated potentials uncovers a series of previously unreported carbon interstitial clusters, from di- to octa-interstitials -- several introducing in-gap states of interest as colour centres -- and shows that their metastability is governed by kinetically accessible pathways rather than energetic ordering. These results chart the interstitial defect landscape and accelerate defect discovery for quantum technologies.

</details>


### [210] [The Heat Kernel Expansion: Curvature for Shock Detection in Higher-Order Financial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19860)
*Mohammad Elsayed,Sara Najem*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于九年间挪威月度财务网络数据，将董事作为节点、公司作为单纯复形的面，利用欧拉示性数、挠率和高阶聚类系数三种拓扑度量分析网络演化，发现曲率（热核展开系数）能灵敏检测法律冲击，而传统拓扑度量无法可靠捕捉网络震荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索拓扑度量能否可靠捕捉金融网络中的外部冲击（如法律变化），并寻找比传统拓扑指标更灵敏的网络震荡检测方法。

Method: 将董事-公司隶属关系建模为单纯复形，计算欧拉示性数（通过贝蒂数）、高阶拉普拉斯矩阵的简化行列式（挠率）、高阶聚类系数，以及热核级数展开系数（曲率）作为几何度量，分析这些指标在九年月度数据中的变化。

Result: 欧拉示性数和挠率未能捕捉法律冲击的影响；曲率指标能灵敏反映立法引起的变化，其拐点对应外部强迫，极小值对应冲击到达时间；挠率分量出现尖锐转变，高阶聚类系数呈现平滑变化。

Conclusion: 并非所有拓扑度量都能可靠捕捉网络冲击；曲率是检测金融网络外部冲击的敏感指标，优于传统拓扑度量如欧拉示性数和挠率。

Abstract: This work follows the evolution of financial networks in Norway over a period of nine years at a monthly rate. The data consist of board directors and their affiliations to companies, which we model as simplicial complexes. In this framework, directors are represented as nodes and companies as faces of the complex. To characterize the latter, we focus on three topological measures: the Euler characteristic, computed through the Betti numbers, torsion computed through the reduced determinant of the higher-order Laplacians, and higher-order clustering coefficients. The first two fail to capture the effect of imposed law on representation, unlike our notion of curvature which is a geometrical measure computed from the coefficients of the series expansion of the heat kernel in powers of time, which is our major contribution in this work. In particular, the Euler characteristic integrates curvature, and thus local information is lost. Subsequently, not every topological measure can reliably capture shocks in networks. Further, the number of spanning trees may undergo significant changes at the lowest order, yet these changes need not be reflected in the torsion. Conversely, the change in the curvature revealed variation in the board interlock due to legislation, and serves as a sensitive measure for detecting shocks in networks. Inflection points in curvature are associated with external forcing, and minima with shock arrival times. Sharp transitions are also observed in the components of torsion, while smooth changes are observed in higher-order clustering.

</details>


### [211] [Advancing Threshold-Inception Modeling for Predictive Simulation of Ionic Wind Fan Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20124)
*Siim Heering,Juri Volodin,Vootele Mets,Rasmus Talviste,Jüri Raud,Karl-Eerik Unt,Indrek Jõgi,Veronika Zadin*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本研究通过实验与仿真对比，验证了阈值起晕多物理场模型对离子风风扇的预测能力，发现电极表面粗糙度是影响模型精度的关键因素，纳入粗糙度后可显著改善定量吻合度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前阈值起晕模型在离子风风扇（EAD风扇）中的应用缺乏系统的实验验证，模型假设（如光滑电极表面）与实际情况存在偏差，亟需评估其预测能力并明确改进方向。

Method: 采用线-筒式电aerodynamic（EAD）风扇作为参考系统，通过可调电极间距设计，对比数值仿真与实验测量的气流特性、放电电流及性能趋势；对发射极表面进行显微表征，并将表面粗糙度引入数值模型进行修正。

Result: 数值仿真与实验在定性上吻合良好，但在高电压和大电极间隙下出现系统性偏差；实验发现发射极表面存在微米级突起，引入表面粗糙度后模型对气流速度的定量预测精度提升。

Conclusion: 阈值起晕模型为EAD风扇仿真提供了稳健基础，但电极表面形态是可靠预测的关键因素；该工作推进了离子风风扇建模方法的验证与精炼，为工程导向仿真工具的开发提供了指导。

Abstract: This study investigates the predictive capability of a threshold inception-based multiphysics modeling approach for ionic wind fans by direct comparison with experimental measurements. A wire-to-cylinder electroaerodynamic (EAD) fan with variable electrode spacing is used as a reference system to assess the model's ability to reproduce airflow characteristics, discharge current, and performance trends under atmospheric conditions. Numerical simulations show good qualitative agreement with experimental results across all tested configurations; however, systematic deviations emerge at higher voltages and larger electrode gaps. Analysis of these discrepancies indicates that the commonly adopted assumption of perfectly smooth emitter surfaces can limit model accuracy. Experimental characterization of the emitter wire reveals micro-scale surface protrusions, which locally enhance the electric field and alter corona inception behavior. Incorporating representative surface roughness into the numerical model improves quantitative agreement with measured airflow velocities. The results demonstrate that while the threshold inception model provides a robust foundation for EAD fan simulations, electrode surface morphology is a critical factor for reliable prediction. This work advances the validation and refinement of ionic wind fan modeling methodologies and identifies key considerations for the development of more accurate engineering-oriented simulation tools.

</details>


### [212] [A Social Force Model of the Evacuation from a Big Box Store](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20136)
*Gavin A. Buxton*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个各向异性社会力模型，使用椭圆截面表示行人、不规则多边形表示轮椅使用者，并加入物理交互、决策能力、小群体、恐慌传播和从众行为。通过模拟大型零售商店的疏散过程，发现忽略"员工专用"出口而仅使用主入口会显著增加平均疏散时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社会力模型通常将行人简化为圆形，无法准确反映人体的各向异性（前后宽度不同）以及轮椅使用者的几何特征；同时缺乏对恐慌传播、从众行为等复杂社会心理因素的综合建模。

Method: 采用椭圆截面表示行人、不规则多边形表示轮椅使用者；建立各向异性社会力模型，包含速度和角度依赖的避碰倾向；基于重叠面积计算法向力和切向力；集成决策能力、小群体行为、恐慌传播和从众机制；在大型超市场景中沿最短路径进行疏散模拟。

Result: 研究发现，疏散过程中忽略"员工专用"出口而仅从主入口撤离会显著增加平均疏散时间。

Conclusion: 模型能更真实地模拟不同人群（包括轮椅使用者）的疏散行为；出口可感知性对疏散效率有重要影响，合理利用所有可用出口能显著缩短疏散时间。

Abstract: We include elliptical cross-sections to physically represent people, and irregular polygons to represent wheelchair users, in an anisotropic social force model whose velocity and angular dependence also captures the social tendency for people to avoid walking into one another. Physical interactions are included that depend on the area of overlap between people, or obstacles, to capture normal forces that resist compression and tangential forces that resist sliding motion. The model is further extended to include decision making capabilities, small social groups, the spread of panic, and herding behavior. A large box store is simulated during an evacuation where people move through the store, along the shortest path, to their desired exits. The effects of exit choice, or the perceived availability of exits, on exit times is elucidated. It is found that ignoring 'staff only' exits, and only exiting from the main entrances, can significantly increase average egress times.

</details>
